		local state = {}
		for propertyId, value in pairs(compactState) do
			local propertyName = PROPERTY_NAMES[tonumber(propertyId)]
			if propertyName then state[propertyName] = value end
		end
		frame.states[id] = state
	end
	frame[1], frame[2], frame[3] = nil, nil, nil
end
local active = false
local playbackAnchor = CFrame.new()
local clones, byId = {}, {}

local function getRootPart()
	local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
	return character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function clean()
	for _, clone in ipairs(clones) do pcall(function() clone:Destroy() end) end
	clones, byId = {}, {}
end

local function prepare(root)
	local allObjects = {root}
	for _, descendant in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do allObjects[#allObjects + 1] = descendant end
	for _, obj in ipairs(allObjects) do
		if obj:IsA("Script") or obj:IsA("LocalScript") or obj:IsA("ModuleScript") then
			obj:Destroy()
		elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
			if not obj:GetAttribute("VFXRecorderViewportPart") then obj.Anchored = true end
			obj.CanCollide, obj.CanTouch, obj.CanQuery = false, false, false
		elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Beam") or obj:IsA("Trail") or obj:IsA("PointLight") or obj:IsA("SpotLight") or obj:IsA("SurfaceLight") or obj:IsA("Fire") or obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Sparkles") or obj:IsA("Highlight") then
			obj.Enabled = false
		elseif obj:IsA("Sound") then
			pcall(function() obj:Stop() end)
		end
	end
end

local function pathFrom(root, obj)
	local names = {}
	local current = obj
	while current and current ~= root do
		table.insert(names, 1, current.Name .. "|" .. current.ClassName)
		current = current.Parent
	end
	return table.concat(names, "/")
end

local function replaceViewports(rootClone)
	local character = player.Character
	if not character then return end
	local all = {rootClone}
	for _, d in ipairs(rootClone:GetDescendants()) do all[#all + 1] = d end
	for _, viewport in ipairs(all) do
		if viewport:IsA("ViewportFrame") then
			local world = viewport:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("WorldModel")
			if world then
				for _, oldModel in ipairs(world:GetChildren()) do
					if oldModel:IsA("Model") and (oldModel:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or oldModel:FindFirstChildOfClass("AnimationController") or oldModel:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Motor6D", true)) then
						local oldArchivable = character.Archivable
						character.Archivable = true
						local newModel = character:Clone()
						character.Archivable = oldArchivable
						local map = {[""] = newModel}
						for _, n in ipairs(newModel:GetDescendants()) do map[pathFrom(newModel, n)] = n end
						local oldList = {oldModel}
						for _, n in ipairs(oldModel:GetDescendants()) do oldList[#oldList + 1] = n end
						for _, old in ipairs(oldList) do
							local id = old:GetAttribute("VFXRecorderId")
							local new = map[pathFrom(oldModel, old)]
							if new and new.ClassName == old.ClassName then
								if id then new:SetAttribute("VFXRecorderId", id) end
								if old:IsA("BasePart") then new:SetAttribute("VFXRecorderViewportPart", true) end
							end
						end
						newModel:PivotTo(oldModel:GetPivot())
						newModel.Parent = world
						oldModel:Destroy()
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

local function build()
	clean()
	for _, info in ipairs(DATA.roots) do
		local template = sourceFolder:FindFirstChild(info.name)
		if template then
			local clone = template:Clone()
			replaceViewports(clone)
			prepare(clone)
			for _, obj in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
				local id = obj:GetAttribute("VFXRecorderId")
				if id then byId[id] = obj end
			end
			local rootId = clone:GetAttribute("VFXRecorderId")
			if rootId then byId[rootId] = clone end
			clone.Parent = nil
			clones[#clones + 1] = clone
			info.clone = clone
		end
	end
end

local function isVFXCarrier(part)
	if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
	for _, d in ipairs(part:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Trail") or d:IsA("Fire") or d:IsA("Smoke") or d:IsA("Sparkles") or d:IsA("PointLight") or d:IsA("SpotLight") or d:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function apply(elapsed)
	if #DATA.frames == 0 then return end
	local index = 1
	while index < #DATA.frames and DATA.frames[index + 1].time <= elapsed do index += 1 end
	local first = DATA.frames[index]
	local second = DATA.frames[math.min(index + 1, #DATA.frames)]
	local alpha = 0
	if second.time > first.time then alpha = math.clamp((elapsed - first.time) / (second.time - first.time), 0, 1) end
	local root = getRootPart()

	for id, props in pairs(first.states) do
		local obj = byId[tonumber(id) or id]
		if obj and obj.Parent then
			local nextProps = second.states[id]
			for property, value in pairs(props) do
				local nextValue = nextProps and nextProps[property]
				local ok, result = pcall(function()
					if property == "CFrame" and obj:IsA("BasePart") and typeof(value) == "CFrame" and not obj:GetAttribute("VFXRecorderViewportPart") then
						local recordedOrigin = DATA.frames[1].playerCFrame or CFrame.new()
						local firstPlayer = first.playerCFrame or recordedOrigin
						local secondPlayer = second.playerCFrame or firstPlayer
						local target, localA, localB

						if FOLLOW_PLAYER then
							target = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -PLAYBACK_OFFSET)
							localA = firstPlayer:ToObjectSpace(value)
							localB = (typeof(nextValue) == "CFrame" and secondPlayer:ToObjectSpace(nextValue)) or localA
						else
							-- Fixed once at start; replay the recorded world-space path.
							target = playbackAnchor * CFrame.new(0, 0, -PLAYBACK_OFFSET)
							localA = recordedOrigin:ToObjectSpace(value)
							localB = (typeof(nextValue) == "CFrame" and recordedOrigin:ToObjectSpace(nextValue)) or localA
						end

						return target * localA:Lerp(localB, alpha)
					elseif property == "CFrame" and obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj:GetAttribute("VFXRecorderViewportPart") and typeof(value) == "CFrame" and typeof(nextValue) == "CFrame" then
						return value:Lerp(nextValue, alpha)
					elseif property == "Transform" and typeof(value) == "CFrame" and typeof(nextValue) == "CFrame" then
						return value:Lerp(nextValue, alpha)
					elseif typeof(value) == "Vector3" and typeof(nextValue) == "Vector3" then
						return value:Lerp(nextValue, alpha)
					elseif typeof(value) == "UDim2" and typeof(nextValue) == "UDim2" then
						return value:Lerp(nextValue, alpha)
					elseif typeof(value) == "Color3" and typeof(nextValue) == "Color3" then
						return value:Lerp(nextValue, alpha)
					elseif typeof(value) == "number" and typeof(nextValue) == "number" then
						return value + (nextValue - value) * alpha
					end
					return value
				end)
				if ok then pcall(function() obj[property] = result end) end
			end
		end
	end
end

local function resolveGuiParent(path)
	local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
	local current = playerGui
	if type(path) == "string" and path ~= "" then
		for name in string.gmatch(path, "[^\1]+") do
			local nextChild = current:FindFirstChild(name)
			if not nextChild then current = nil break end
			current = nextChild
		end
	end
	if current and current ~= playerGui then return current end
	local fallback = playerGui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ScreenGui")
	if not fallback then
		fallback = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
		fallback.Name = "VFXRecorderPlaybackGui"
		fallback.ResetOnSpawn = false
		fallback.Parent = playerGui
	end
	return fallback
end

local function play()
	if active then return end
	if #DATA.frames == 0 then warn("No recorded VFX frames") return end
	active = true
	build()
	playbackAnchor = getRootPart().CFrame
	local start = os.clock()
	local connection
	connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		if not active then connection:Disconnect() return end
		local elapsed = os.clock() - start
		for _, info in ipairs(DATA.roots) do
			if info.clone and not info.spawned and elapsed >= info.birth then
				info.spawned = true
				info.clone.Parent = info.gui and (info.name and info.guiParentPath ~= "" and resolveGuiParent(info.guiParentPath) or player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")) or workspace
			end
			if info.clone and info.spawned and not info.removed and info.death and elapsed >= info.death then
				info.removed = true
				info.clone:Destroy()
			end
		end
		apply(elapsed)
		if elapsed >= DATA.duration then
			active = false
			connection:Disconnect()
			clean()
		end
	end)
end

play()
